fix only（1 epoch + clean data, LoRA α=128/r=64, lr=2e-5）\n- mvp2_rsft_r2b：data fix + training dynamics fix（1 epoch + clean data, LoRA α=32/r=64, lr=1e-5）\n- mvp2_rsft_train_r1_s43：r1 seed=43 variance metrics 后统一汇报。\n\n### 4. 📋 待办\n- Worker analysis 返回 r2a/r2b metrics → update_registry（conclusion + metrics + checkpoint_path），判断 pass/negative → 按 on-experiment-done 分叉处理\n- r2a 或 r2b 任一 pass（rel_f1 > 0.4057）→ RSFT 路线存活，向 Director 汇报成功 + 建议扩大训练（full epoch sweep / 更多 seeds）\n- r2a 和 r2b 全 negative → RSFT 全线确认死亡，向 Director 汇报 + 立即提交 mvp2_ced_reranker_poc（CED-as-reranker）到空闲 GPU\n- r1_s43 也需要更新 registry（大概率 negative，与 r1 同参数）\n- Worker reranker code 返回 → 检查代码质量 → submit_on_allocated 或 submit_training_job 到 cuda:0\n- CED reranker 完成后 → 若 rel_f1 > 0.4057，MVP-2 通过，进入 paper 阶段；否则需要 oracle reranker 分析 ceiling\n- 4 张 GPU 全空 + 无 RUNNING 实验 = **资源失职状态**，Worker 返回后必须立即铺满\n\n### 5. 🎯 战略上下文\n**决策链**：GRPO+QLoRA 判死 (D031/D033) → RSFT pivot (D031) → RSFT r1 catastrophic fail rel_f1=0.0483 (D037) ## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- GPU 白名单：cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3（全在 rtx5090-4x）。无其他服务器可用
- 运行目录必须是 `/root/autodl-tmp/`，不能 cd 到 freige/ 子目录（会导致 import 找到内层空包）
- RSFT 训练禁止 `--quantize`（4-bit merge_and_unload 在 RTX 5090 有 CUBLAS 错误），bf16 即可
- Qwen3 generation_config 会静默覆盖 temperature/top_k，生成前必须显式 override
- W&B entity/project 未在 project config 中配置，fetch_wandb_metrics 会失败——只能通过 SSH 读日志获取指标
- SFT baseline: rel_f1=0.4057, evi_f1=0.798——所有实验 pass/fail 以此为基准线

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：rtx5090-4x，账户 root，4× RTX 5090 32GB
- 工作目录：/root/autodl-tmp/freige（代码），/root/autodl-tmp/（运行目录）
- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python，激活：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`
- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B（基座），/root/autodl-tmp/sft_output（SFT adapter）
- NLI 模型（CED）：/root/autodl-tmp/models/deberta-v3-large-zeroshot-v2.0
- 数据：/root/autodl-tmp/data/docred（dev/train），/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered/rsft_train_clean.jsonl（CED 过滤后训练数据 2548 samples）
- HF cache：/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache
- RSFT 输出：rsft_output（r1）, rsft_output_r2a, rsft_output_r2b, rsft_output_s43
- 活跃进程：**无**——4 张 GPU 全部空闲，无 tmux session，无 cron

### 3. 📊 当前状态
**刚做完**：Session 初始化（registry + reconcile + ROADMAP）。发现 mvp2_rsft_r2b（cron 回调触发）和 mvp2_rsft_r2a（reconcile 发现）都进入 ANALYZING。mvp2_rsft_train_r1_s43 也在 ANALYZING（seed 变体，07:37 完成但未处理）。

**正在等待的 Worker**：
1. `worker_analysis_rsft_r2ab_20260515T093729`：SSH 读取 r2a/r2b/r1_s43 日志，提取 eval metrics（rel_f1/evi_f1 等），对比 SFT baseline
2. `worker_code_ced_reranker_poc_20260515T093913`：编写 CED-as-reranker 推理脚本（ced_reranker.py），dry-run 验证

**Registry ANALYZING**：
- mvp2_rsft_r2a：data fix only（1 epoch + clean data, LoRA α=128/r=64, lr=2e-5）
- mvp2_rsft_r2b：data fix + training dynamics fix（1 epoch + clean data, LoRA α=32/r=64, lr=1e-5）
- mvp2_rsft_train_r1_s43：r1 seed=43 variance estimate（同 r1 参数，3 epochs）

**QUEUED**：mvp2_ced_reranker_poc（CED-as-reranker，已批准等 GPU）
**PENDING**：mvp2_rsft_ced_scoring_topk3（top_k=3 ablation，等人工审批）

**尚未向 Director 汇报本轮结果**——等 Worker 返回 metrics 后统一汇报。

### 4. 📋 待办
- Worker analysis 返回 r2a/r2b metrics → update_registry（conclusion + metrics + checkpoint_path），判断 pass/negative → 按 on-experiment-done 分叉处理
- r2a 或 r2b 任一 pass（rel_f1 > 0.4057）→ RSFT 路线存活，向 Director 汇报成功 + 建议扩大训练（full epoch sweep / 更多 seeds）
- r2a 和 r2b 全 negative → RSFT 全线确认死亡，向 Director 汇报 + 立即提交 mvp2_ced_reranker_poc（CED-as-reranker）到空闲 GPU
- r1_s43 也需要更新 registry（大概率 negative，与 r1 同参数）
- Worker reranker code 返回 → 检查代码质量 → submit_on_allocated 或 submit_training_job 到 cuda:0
- CED reranker 完成后 → 若 rel_f1 > 0.4057，MVP-2 通过，进入 paper 阶段；否则需要 oracle reranker 分析 ceiling
- 4 张 GPU 全空 + 无 RUNNING 实验 = **资源失职状态**，Worker 返回后必须立即铺满

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
**决策链**：GRPO+QLoRA 判死 (D031/D033) → RSFT pivot (D031) → RSFT r1 catastrophic fail rel_f1=0.0483 (D037) → r2a/r2b 诊断实验 + CED-as-reranker pivot

**核心判断点**：r2a/r2b 的结果决定两条路：
- 如果 LoRA scaling + lr 是根因（r2b pass, r2a fail）→ RSFT 可救，conservative params 训练
- 如果数据清洗就够（r2a pass）→ RSFT 问题是数据质量，不是训练 dynamics
- 如果全 fail → 训练路线彻底判死，CED-as-reranker 是唯一出路

**易错点**：
- 不要假设 r1_s43 结果与 r1 不同——同参数不同 seed，大概率同样 negative，但必须确认
- CED reranker 需要在 **dev set** 上生成 candidates（不是复用 train set 的 rsft_gen_s0-s3 输出）
- D037 说 "pivot 到 CED-as-reranker" 但 r2a/r2b 是后续诊断实验——如果 r2a/r2b 成功，pivot 决策可能需要 Director 重新评估
- report.md 是自动生成的，不要手动编辑——update_registry 后系统自动刷新

    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

collapse confirmed → KILLED at step ~30**:\\\\n- step 5: frac_zero_std=0.2, f1=0.016（初始信号）\\\\n- step 10-30: frac_zero_std=0.7-0.8, f1_reward=0（完全 collapse）\\\\n- reward 全部来自 format_reward (~0.29)，模型放弃 extraction\\\\n- 对比 ced_kl001_r4 同期在 step 20-40 恢复（frac→0.0, f1→0.240），extract_r4 无恢复信号\\\\n\\\\n**科学价值**：证明 extraction-only reward（format+f1, 无 CED）在 D020 配置下无法维持 GRPO 多样性。CED reward Fix: kl_coef=0.0 in r2 retries.\\\"}, \\\"mvp2_grpo_ced_r3\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T11:47:53.099Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T12:09:23.005Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"CED reward with balanced weights (format×0.3, f1×3.0, ced×1.0) + stable training (num_iterations=1, KL early stop at 100) + kl_coef=0.05 improves Evi-F1 over SFT baseline\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"KL divergence explosion at step 5: first pass KL=4.4M, second pass KL=69.5B (threshold=100). KL early stopping correctly triggered, model saved but no useful training occurred.\\\\n\\\\nRoot cause: single-GPU training without --gradient_accumulation_steps (effective batch=1) → extremely noisy gradients → policy diverged from reference in 5 steps. Combined with QLoRA merge rounding amplifying initial policy-reference gap.\\\\n\\\\nKey metrics at step 5: loss=269987, kl=4.4M, entropy=0.084 (near 0), format_reward=0.10, f1_reward=0.05, ced_reward=0.15, grad_norm=0.16. EXIT_CODE=129 is artifact of KL early stop exit path.\\\\n\\\\nerror_type: hyperparameter | can_auto_fix: false\\\\nFix options: (1) add --gradient_accumulation_steps 16 for effective batch ~16; (2) reduce lr from 5e-5 to 5e-6; (3) increase kl_coef from 0.05 to 0.5; (4) lr warmup steps\\\"}, \\\"mvp2_grpo_ced_r2\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T10:58:11.663Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T11:31:49.724Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"killed\\\"}, \\\"mvp2_grpo_ced_r1\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T08:02:41.858Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T10:37:23.856Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"killed\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Mode collapse diagnosed at step 80/189 (F1=0.001, format reward hacking). Root causes and fixes applied to grpo_trainer.py:\\\\n\\\\n(A) Checkpoint: save_strategy was \\\\\\\"epoch\\\\\\\" → now supports --save_steps/--save_total_limit CLI args, defaults to steps-based saving. Validated: checkpoint-1/2 saved in dry-run.\\\\n\\\\n(B) Reward weighting: all 3 rewards (format/f1/ced) were equally summed by TRL → added WeightedRewardFn wrapper + --format_reward_weight/--f1_reward_weight/--ced_reward_weight CLI args. Dry-run confirmed weighting works (format raw=0.1 × w=0.3 → 0.03).\\\\n\\\\n(C) Clip ratio: num_iterations=1 (default) meant single pass, ratio≡1, clipping never activated → added --num_iterations CLI arg (set to 2 in r2). Also added --max_steps, --kl_coef (beta). Dry-run clip_ratio=0 expected (zero reward variance → zero advantage → no update needed).\\\\n\\\\n(D) Added --save_total_limit CLI arg (previously hardcoded to 2, now configurable).\\\\n\\\\nDry-run validated: 2 steps completed, checkpoints saved, no OOM with num_iterations=2 on 4xRTX5090. completions/clipped_ratio=1.0 (all hit max_completion_length=1024) — expected for cold-start without warmup.\\\"}, \\\"mvp2_grpo_ced\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T07:10:31.165Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T08:00:02.874Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\":
